## Implementation note 2026-09-02 — batch 220, P-0336 SCB 1920

- Opened the previously untried Sollentuna 1920 SCB parish-book extract in

  identify `SE/RA/420401/10/H 1 AA/13`, permanent id
  `oteW3OgRrH6d0G02H087k3`.
- The `A0056304` manifest places Sollentuna at image 294 and Lovö at image
  340; the alternative `C0301345` reproduction places them at 589 and 681.
  Full originals delimit Sollentuna pages 1–90 to the 46 source images
  `A0056304_00294`–`_00339`; image 340 begins Lovö page 1.
- Downloaded all 46 full originals through the signed-in viewer and ran two
  local Apple Vision OCR navigation passes on an observed Apple M4 Max/
  arm64, including 276 name/occupation fields. Five plausible nominations
  were read in full originals and rejected as other names, birth profiles or
  occupations. No visually checked nomination is Olaus Fredberg.
- Preserved S-0625/C-0809/A-3094–A-3095, both manifests, the OCR candidate
  file, structured observation, five candidate copies, three boundary
  copies, and all 46 screened full originals in the repository. The batch
  is therefore independent of Chrome, Downloads and chat history. OCR can
  miss handwriting and this was not a name-by-name reading, so the result
  is not a person, residence, parish, year or whole-reproduction null. No
  person or relationship was created.
- Do not repeat blind OCR of `A0056304_00294`–`_00339` without a new address,
  farm, page, family-member or occupation key. T-0012 remains
  `ONGOING`/`DOING`; next breadth rotation is P-0051. No reading-room/copy
  order, PDF, deploy, commit or push was created.
- Regenerated the media manifest and dashboard data. Validation observed 500
  people, 2,576 assertions, 525 parent links, 1,951 Markdown records and

  2,521 exact, 1,771 source-scoped, 28 citation-scoped and zero unlinked.

## Implementation note 2026-09-02 — batch 217, P-0336 census 1900

- Filled P-0336's previously overlooked central census gap between 1890 and
  1910. Riksarkivet records `Folk_117700509`–`Folk_117700515` place the
  married station foreman Olaus, Johanna and children Frida, Augusta, Ester,
  Edit and Oskar at Jordgubben N:o 11, Kungsholm rote 17, in 1900.
- Read linked original `Folk_001017-164`. It confirms the household roles,
  Olaus's occupation and birth-year sequence 1852, 1866, 1883, 1890, 1891,
  1894 and 1895. Children's surnames are not constructed, and the source
  alone does not make Johanna Frida's biological mother.
- Preserved a real conflict for Edit: 1900's register and original both say
  1894, while 1910 says 1895. Oskar is already born 1895 in 1900, so no twin
  claim is supported. Edit's own birth record is required before resolution.

  catalogue opened all seven person records without a new challenge, and the

- Regenerated the media manifest and dashboard data. Validation observed 500

## Implementation note 2026-09-02 — batch 209, P-0133 Flen–Helgesta

- Continued the breadth-first rotation using only Riksarkivet. JSON-LD
  separated Flen's parish volumes from the municipal books and routed A II
  a/2 a (`F0003350`) and A II a/3 c (`F0003354`). Helgesta A II a/2 is
  `SE/ULA/10394/A II a/2`, reproduction `F0003387`.
- Flen A II a/2 a page 216 follows Erik Karlsson, Matilda Charlotta
  Sjöberg, foster son Sven Arvid and son Karl Oskar from Brosätter to page
  185 on 1907-11-13. Page 185, Segerslund, carries the same four-person
  household to the continuation book on 1907-11-28.
- Flen A II a/3 c page 224 continues Erik, Matilda and Sven Arvid to Wäsby
  page 255 on 1908-10-30. Karl Oskar is absent only from that named later
  household; no destination is inferred. Page 255 records departure to
  Helgesta on 1909-01-18; the rerun of Helgesta B/4 later corrected the

**Status**: ONGOING | **Size**: L
**Phase**: DOING

## Context

The project has not established true source exhaustion on any complete branch.
The approved next-edition target is now one balanced generation beyond the
first Adam/Axel report: 32 theoretical ancestor positions, all now represented
by named people. P-0253 Erik Jonas Henriksson's former openings are P-0338
Henrik Henriksson and P-0339 Margareta Charlotta Sjödin. The two former
positions behind P-0287 Johannes Ivar Fredberg are P-0336 Olaus Fredberg and
P-0337 Brita Stina. The task must both resolve as many open relationships as
the sources allow and turn the whole known
cohort into rich, source-led lives before research moves deeper.

T-0011 is complete. T-0014 established the wife's separate proband and
depth-1 frontier, then reached a source-specific external gate. Under
`PCR-2026-08-21-003` that gate remains visible on T-0014 but no longer blocks
this executable recovery cohort.

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] For every one of the 32 named people in the next shared depth-5 edition
  cohort, complete the applicable central spine and attempt every relevant
  Riksarkivet source family in the coverage matrix, recording exact scope,
  result, provenance and uncertainty.
- [x] Give all four originally open parent positions behind P-0253 and P-0287
  verified people or exact source-specific terminal states and reactivation
  conditions. P-0287's positions are P-0336–P-0337 and P-0253's are
  P-0338–P-0339.
- [ ] Enforce breadth before depth: no P-0123/Motala continuation or other
  deeper-tip expansion may outrun the balanced next-edition cohort.
- [ ] Systematically use applicable death/burial material, SCB extracts,
  parish extracts and censuses to complete life events and triangulate
  identities.
- [ ] Use applicable probate, tax/mantals, court, parish-meeting and
  poor-relief records to recover parents, heirs, guardians, residences,
  property, paternity clues and household context.
- [ ] Use military, property/land and seamen's-house sources when occupation,
  residence or status supplies a concrete trigger.
- [ ] Reopen any branch supported by new evidence; otherwise replace broad
  terminal labels with exact, source-family-specific coverage and an
  observable reactivation condition.
- [ ] Give every newly named relative or relationship-bearing person a P-id
  and preserve relevant biographical facts, images, citations, conflicts,
  negative searches and checksums under the repository model.
- [ ] Keep both the direct pedigree and discovered kin synchronized and pass
  the full genealogy validation and bounded ancestor audit.
- [ ] Produce a durable edition-readiness checkpoint for the complete cohort,
  then stop for a substantial owner discussion of scope, narrative, visual
  design and evidence presentation before any manifest, PDF task or rendering.

## Verification Plan

### Hard Endpoints

- [ ] `node scripts/ancestor-audit.mjs --through-depth=5` - verifies that
  reopened and still-terminal tips remain structurally coherent.
- [ ] `node scripts/validate-genealogy.mjs` - verifies identifiers, links,
  citations, media checksums and Wotan JSON.
- [ ] `jq empty wotan/backlog.json` - verifies valid backlog JSON.

### Manual Checks

- [ ] Compare every matrix-marked high-relevance source family with the
  research log and person dossier for an explicit result or access incident.
- [ ] Review each new relationship independently against the cited source
  image; do not promote name matches alone.
- [ ] Confirm that no Bernhard–Arne evidence search was introduced.

### Risks

- Source-family scope can become unbounded; constrain execution to the fixed
  next-edition cohort and record deferred leads without following them deeper.
- Sparse or indexed records can suggest false identity matches; require a
  multi-field identity chain or retain `LEAD`/`CONFLICT` status.
- UI-only sources can fail outside authenticated sessions; surface the
  incident immediately and preserve a browser/manual reactivation route.

## Approach

Work breadth-first in small source-family batches across the fixed
next-edition cohort. First close central-spine gaps evenly across both sons'
lines, then apply the matrix-marked censuses, extracts, probate, tax/mantals,
court, parish/poor-relief and conditional occupational sources to build rich
portraits. Rotate across people and source families so an easy branch cannot
run a generation ahead. After each batch, synchronize dossiers, citations,
media, frontier and the coverage matrix. The final research checkpoint is an
owner gate, not authorization to create the next PDF.

## Obstacles

- No task-level blocker. The Riksarkivet records API still has a client/WAF
  access split (`ERR_BLOCKED_BY_CLIENT` in Chrome; WAF 403 separately), and
  direct unauthenticated IIIF has returned 403. The authenticated NAD UI and
  its session-bound full-image download are now working alternatives after a
  fresh login. Long batches and a stale download panel can silently repeat a
  prior image, so future runs must verify displayed image-id metadata before
  counting a download. Gryt C/1–C/4 remain a real non-digitization gap. None
  of these conditions may be converted to a source-negative conclusion.
- P-0058's relevant mantal originals are now exactly identified but all four
  Riksarkivet posts are `Läsesal`. FamilySearch may distribute the pre-1860
  films, but the current session reached an authentication gate before the
  exact DGS/camera restriction could be verified. Reactivate after
  FamilySearch login, via archive copy order/reading room, or after new
  digitization; this is not a task-level blocker because other cohort work
  remains executable.

  Lagfors by. I Ljustorps arkiv `SE/HLA/1010115` lästes A I/10:s hela
  Lagforsavsnitt s. 248–265 utan säker Erik Jonas, som ett ortavgränsat
  arbetsresultat och inte ett helvolymsnoll. A I/9:s Lagforsavsnitt s.
  206–224 gav den positiva träffen: drängen Erik Jonas Henriksson på s. 221
  flyttades reciprok till s. 222 den 1881-11-10 och skrivs där som arbetare.
  Båda raderna anger 1860-01-23 i Ljustorp. Med detta sökankare återlästes
  C/3 s. 114: Erik Jonas föddes 23 januari, döptes 24 januari och var son
  till torparen P-0338 Henrik Henriksson i Lagfors och P-0339 Margareta
  Charlotta Sjödin, 34 år. C-0272/A-1739 har rättats men bevarats som
  revisionsspår; senare 1860-02-28 står kvar som konflikt A-1532. S-0258,

  original bevarar kedjan. Alla 32 utgåvepositioner är därmed namngivna.

  för att direkt följa P-0338–P-0339 en generation djupare. T-0012 förblir
  `ONGOING` i fas `DOING`; ingen PDF-produktion har påbörjats.
  Batchkontrollen passerar med 1 428 påståenden, 938 Markdownposter, 856
  mediefiler och giltig Wotan-JSON. Huvudrevisionen har väntad icke-nollstatus
  med tio ogiltiga slutstatusar. Kristinas djup-4-revision visar nu 30 kända
  anor och 16 av 16 namngivna positioner; samtliga 16 spetsar är öppna för
  den fortsatta breddberikningen.

  1731–1913, så 1922 ligger utanför just den registertäckningen. Sättna hörde
  då till Medelpads västra domsagas tingslag. Riksarkivets öppna serieträd
  för `SE/HLA/1040237` visar `F II Bouppteckningar`, och ArkivDigitals
  publika katalog identifierar 1922 års volym som `F II a/9`, AID
  `v510406`. Själva akten lästes inte: Riksarkivets arkivpost stoppades av
  ALTCHA, som inte automatiserades, och ArkivDigitals bildvisare krävde
  inloggning. S-0418, C-0528 och A-2351–A-2352 bevarar därför katalogvägen,
  åtkomsthindret och en exakt manuell återstart utan att påstå att Abraham
  finns eller saknas i volymen. Inga personer, relationer eller mediefiler
  skapades. T-0012 förblir `ONGOING`/`DOING`.

  JSON-LD, OAI-EAD och IIIF öppnade däremot Göta livgardes församlings
  B II/1, `SE/SSA/0005H/B II/1`, reproduktion `00025680`. Katalogen anger
  att längden endast omfattar personer som inte är upptagna i
  rekrytrullorna. Hela 1876-intervallet, bilderna 95–101, lästes i
  fulloriginal: de åtta förda inflyttningsposterna står på bild 95 och
  följande inflyttningssidor är tomma. Ingen säker Olaus/Olof
  Fredberg/Fredriksson finns. Resultatet är ett komplett B II/1-årsnoll,
  inte person-, Stockholms-, förbands- eller tjänstgöringsnoll. Tillsammans
  med B I/2:s tidigare namnformsnoll försvagas Göta-församlingshypotesen,
  men annat förband, annan namnform, kort/utelämnad tjänst och bristande
  bokföring förblir öppna. S-0560/C-0726/A-2823–A-2825, sju fulloriginal,
  tre metadataoriginal och tre åtkomstsvar bevarar resultatet. Ingen person
  eller relation skapades. T-0012 förblir `ONGOING`/`DOING`; nästa
  breddrotation är P-0423. Kontroll: 2 306 påståenden, 1 773
  Markdownposter och 3 040 mediafiler inklusive `.gitkeep`; manifest 3 039
  (1 796 exakt/1 215 käll-/28 citationsavgränsade, 0 olänkade) och
  dashboard 471/2 306/479. Data-, parser-, pedigree-, djup-5-,
  utgåvemanifest-, dashboard-, produktionsbygg-, JSON-, manifest-,
  checksumme- och diffkontroller passerade. Auditerna har fortsatt elva
  öppna slutstatusar genom P-0004:s djup 5 och exakt P-0336 öppen genom
  P-0210:s djup 4. Ingen PDF, deployment, commit eller push skapades.
